## Methodology and limits

- Each metric is the median of its own repetition-level measurements. Medians of component times need not sum to the median total. The JSON `folds` field is the fixture's Nova comparison bucket; direct systems do not fold.
- Deterministic fixture seed: `0x5341525a4b04000b`. Fixture generation only; the prover retains its own fresh proof randomness.
- Each system/T/repetition is a newly spawned child process; peak RSS therefore belongs to that one worker process.
- The per-entry baseline emits T+1 proofs: a combined START plus first STEP proof, generic STEP proofs, and a FINAL proof. Setup creates each distinct shape/key once (two shapes when T=1); raw per-proof setup charges that cost to the first proof using that shape and records zero for reuse.
- The reported candidate-set size is a structural fixture count: 16 synthetic receipts with the same issuer, policy, and public bucket. It is weak, separate from log leaves, and not a measured deployment anonymity set. The published deterministic fixture seed exposes fixture witnesses, so these benchmarks make no anonymity or privacy demonstration.
- A configuration may be skipped only when a smaller completed configuration for the same system yields a measured linear T extrapolation above the 90-minute all-repetitions budget. Such a record is explicitly labeled an estimate and contains no invented measurement metrics.
- Direct Spartan baseline APIs do not add the random satisfying-instance fold required for the profile zero-knowledge construction; no zero-knowledge guarantee is claimed for those baselines.
- The per-entry baseline lacks authenticated chaining and the secure-receipt start/end composition; it is intentionally cheaper than a secure composition.
- No git revision is collected. Per-worker stdout and stderr are retained under `raw/` and named by system, T, and repetition.

**Candidate-set warning:** the reported 16-or-fewer figure is a weak structural count of synthetic receipts sharing issuer, policy, and public bucket. The deterministic published fixture seed exposes witnesses, so it is not an anonymity or privacy demonstration; log leaves are not an anonymity-set claim.

Full raw repetition-level values, all individual per-proof values, and paths to child stdout/stderr artifacts are in `results.json`. Constraint-count semantics: folded primary/secondary are Nova public-parameter augmented counts; direct primary is raw standalone entry-branch count and secondary is not applicable; monolithic is raw whole-application count excluding 18 direct wrapper input/output equalities; standalone branches are raw START/STEP/FINAL counts excluding wrappers; uniform is the common application relation count.
